# Step 4 consult — dedup/family rules produced unsound proposals (Claude+Codex, read-only)

You are the Codex side of a structured consult per the signed-off adjudication shape (execution plan §5; operating-contract item 4 makes dedup/family/batch granularity a Claude+Codex call, NOT a user gate — this is not conceptual). Read-only sandbox: verify against the files, propose, don't edit. End with a `## CONSULT VERDICT` section, per-question AGREE / DISAGREE-with-alternative, grounded in files you open.

## What happened

Step 4 built Phase B tooling. The CODE passed a clean review (both load-bearing checks — `phase-b-ingest` rejects any origin/constraint claim without a `notes/` citation; `benchmark-ingest` rejects any entry without a real source citation — are correctly enforced). But the two read-only PROPOSALS the tooling generated are unsound. The agreed D1 process placed a clean-agent review BEFORE applying the alias map precisely to catch this; it fired.

Files (all under `/var/projects/toy_physics/`, read-only):
- `research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_dedup_proposal.yaml` — 140 alias groups, 291 aliases, 631 canonicals, 342 ambiguous.
- `research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_family_map.yaml` — 1358 families, 1329 singletons, 0 unmapped (marked `pre_alias_map`).
- `research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/fit_insertion_points.yaml` — human-readable candidate union.
- Tooling: `.claude/skills/adversarial-audit/lib/core.py` (dedup `build_dedup_proposal` ~:635 + helpers `records_value_compatible` ~:306, `citations_adjacent` ~:284; family `build_family_map` ~:857).

## Finding 1 — dedup over-merges (orchestrator-VERIFIED on stage073)

The alias group `fit_stage073_Lambda_ell_fixed_37` (8 members, `_dedup_proposal.yaml` ~line 2245) merges SEVEN distinct parameters at different lines: `epsilon_r=0.05=1/20` (line 30), aspect ratio (line 48), `Lambda_ell` (line 72), `eta` (line 94), plus `k_m`, `t_x`, `Lambda_star` — span lines 6–94. These are NOT the same physical insertion point. The group's own `adjacency_rule` text says "line distance <= 3", but the operative merge is the `records_value_compatible` path: same `anchor_stage` + overlapping `parameter_family` union + no *conflicting* extracted literal value — and most members carry `literal_values: []`, so nothing conflicts and everything in the stage's parameter soup transitively merges. A clean review agent found 127/140 groups (91%) span >3 lines; ≥4-member groups (stage073=8, stage098=7, stage111=4, stage247=12, stage026=4) systematically collapse distinct fitted values. The sound merges are the small 2-member ones (paper-vs-script-vs-pass2 of the *same* value, e.g. stage026 κ_n=√2/((n+½)π); path-prefix dupes; mechanical-key vs agent-key for the same (stage,param,value)).

**A false merge is the dangerous error**: aliases inherit the canonical's Phase C verdict, so e.g. `epsilon_r=1/20`, the `eta`-pin, and the 37/20 aspect ratio would all get ONE verdict — a real fit could hide behind another candidate's PASS.

## Finding 2 — alias/ambiguous self-contradiction

~153 candidate ids appear in BOTH the alias-merge and the ambiguous-keep-separate sections (e.g. stage026 `g_u=1/2` and `k_req=54/5` are declared aliases into `fit_stage026_K_req_solved_exactly_from_target` AND members of a stage-026 ambiguous group flagged "conflicting literal values → stay separate"). The ambiguous bucket's call is the correct one; the alias merge is wrong. No apply-time tiebreak exists.

## Finding 3 — family map fragments every headline chain into singletons

Family key = normalized `parameter_names` set + EACH extracted literal value. Consequences: (a) the same conceptual parameter at a different stage/value forms a NEW family, so the load-bearing cross-stage chains fragment — 54/5 quadrupole, 37/20 aspect cascade, and Sigma0_can/T_can are all scattered across singleton families; the Σ0 **…867 vs …876 digit-transposition** carriers land in SEPARATE families (and the two purpose-built digit-variant candidates have empty value-keys), so the exact drift the audit targets is invisible at family level; (b) noisy multi-name candidates explode — `fit_stage_105_chi_q` maps to 18 families, `fit_stage_099_k0_sym`→13. Only `chi_Q=1` (`fam_0332`, 12 members) clustered well. Root cause is upstream: Phase A `parameter_names` are whole-sentence symbol captures (one candidate carries ~28 names), so the param-set+value key is both too sparse (values empty) and too noisy (param unions).

## Claude's proposed fix (verify and refine)

**Q1 — Confirm Finding 1.** Open `_dedup_proposal.yaml`, verify stage073 (and one more ≥4-member group, e.g. stage247 or stage098) are false merges, and confirm the operative path is `records_value_compatible` over a `parameter_family` union, not the advertised 3-line adjacency. AGREE the dedup map is unsafe to apply as-is?

**Q2 — Dedup fix = strict, value-anchored, conservative.** Re-spec `dedup-propose`: alias two candidates ONLY when same `anchor_stage` AND same single normalized target parameter AND same extracted literal value AND (identical `(path,line)` OR same normalized path within ≤3 lines OR a pure path-prefix variant). DROP the `records_value_compatible` no-conflicting-value fallback entirely. Any candidate with empty `literal_values`, or with a multi-parameter (polluted) name set that can't be reduced to one target, is NOT merged → it stays a separate canonical (and the alias/ambiguous self-contradiction disappears because nothing is both). Expect the canonical count to rise substantially (toward ~800+); accept that — over-merge hides fits, under-merge only costs redundant Phase C audits. AGREE?

**Q3 — Family fix = conceptual-parameter identity, value as attribute.** Re-spec `family-build`: derive ONE primary conceptual parameter per candidate from the `candidate_key` / `reason` (e.g. `fit_stage073_eta_pinned_37`→`eta`; `fit_stage_105_chi_q`→`chi_Q`) rather than the polluted `parameter_names` union; key the family on that normalized conceptual parameter ALONE (NOT param+value); carry each occurrence's literal value as a per-member ATTRIBUTE so cross-stage occurrences LINK and value divergences (Σ0 867 vs 876) surface as a within-family `value_divergence` finding. One primary family per candidate (kill the 18-way explosion); a small number of genuinely multi-target candidates may map to >1. Remaining singletons in the long tail are acceptable (genuinely distinct single insertions). AGREE?

**Q4 — Seed the known chains?** The D3 consult already enumerated the headline chains (54/5+gamma_GR; 37/20→F1 incl. 4107; chi_Q/m0_hat/N_Q; Sigma0_can/T_can incl. 867/876; barrier 222–224 incl. V_known; calibration 245–253 incl. lambda_L+CODATA; wall-action+m̂/P0). Should `family-build` seed these as explicit named families (a safety net so they cannot fragment), with mechanical membership filled by the conceptual-parameter rule and any candidate the rule misses flagged for review — or do you prefer to trust the re-keyed rule alone and verify coverage post-hoc? Recommend one.

**Q5 — Anything missed.** Is purely-mechanical grouping the right tool here given the upstream `parameter_names` noise, or should one step be agent-assisted? Should we instead fix the Phase A `parameter_names` pollution at the source (re-derive a single target parameter per candidate) before re-running dedup/family — and if so, mechanically or via agents? Flag anything that should change before I write the rebuild directive.

        return self.artifact_root / "benchmarks.yaml"

    @property
    def batches_path(self) -> Path:
        return self.artifact_root / "BATCHES.md"

    def config_path_value(self, key: str) -> Path:
        value = self.adv_value(key)
        if value is None:
            raise SystemExit(f"error: missing adversarial config key: {key}")
        return self.resolve_project_path(str(value))

    def rel(self, path: Path) -> str:
        path = path.resolve()
        try:
            return str(path.relative_to(self.project_root))
        except ValueError:
            try:
                return str(path.relative_to(self.repo_root))
            except ValueError:
                return str(path)

    def abs_from_rel(self, path_text: str) -> Path:
        p = Path(path_text)
        if p.is_absolute():
            return p
        candidate = self.project_root / p
        if candidate.exists() or not str(path_text).startswith(("docs/", "graph/")):
            return candidate
        return self.repo_root / p


def ensure_tree(env: Env) -> None:
    env.artifact_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name in REQUIRED_DIRS:
        (env.artifact_root / name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def initial_manifest(env: Env) -> dict[str, Any]:
    ts = now_iso()
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "project_name": env.adv.get("project_name", env.cfg.get("project", {}).get("name", "pde_ledger")),
        "artifact_root": env.adv.get("artifact_root", "redteam_adversarial"),
        "created_at": ts,
        "updated_at": ts,
        "state_machine": STATE_MACHINE,
        "candidates": {},
        "dry_runs": {},
    }


def load_manifest(env: Env) -> dict[str, Any]:
    m = load_yaml(env.manifest_path)
    if not m:
        return initial_manifest(env)
    m.setdefault("state_machine", STATE_MACHINE)
    m.setdefault("candidates", {})
    m.setdefault("dry_runs", {})
    return m


def save_manifest(env: Env, manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    manifest["updated_at"] = now_iso()
    write_yaml(env.manifest_path, manifest)


def transition(entry: dict[str, Any], status: str) -> None:
    if status not in STATE_MACHINE:
        raise SystemExit(f"error: invalid candidate status: {status}")
    entry["status"] = status
    entry.setdefault("status_timestamps", {})[status] = now_iso()

def add_param(params: list[str], value: str | None) -> None:
    if value and value not in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
        params.append(value)


def detect_parameters(text: str) -> list[str]:
    params: list[str] = []
    for match in re.finditer(r"`([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:_[A-Za-z0-9]+)+)`", text):
        add_param(params, normalized_param_name(match.group(1)))
    for match in re.finditer(r"\b([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:_[A-Za-z0-9]+)+)\b", text):
        add_param(params, normalized_param_name(match.group(1)))
    for match in re.finditer(r"\\([A-Za-z]+)(?:_\{?([^}\s^]+)\}?)?", text):
        command, subscript = match.groups()
        if command in TEX_COMMAND_DENYLIST:
            continue
        if not subscript and command in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
            continue
        if subscript:
            add_param(params, normalized_param_name(f"{command}_{subscript}"))
        elif command not in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
            add_param(params, normalized_param_name(command))
    return sorted(set(params))


def candidate_parameters(text: str) -> list[str]:
    params = detect_parameters(text)
    lower = text.lower()
    has_dtn = "dtn" in lower or "fingerprint" in lower
    has_value = bool(re.search(r"(\\frac\{|\d+\s*/\s*\d+|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|=)", text))
    is_value_claim = has_fit_signal(text)
    if has_dtn and has_value and is_value_claim:
        params.append("matched_fingerprint_value")
    if params and is_value_claim:
        return sorted(set(params))
    return []


def candidate_key_for(stage: str, text: str) -> str:
    params = candidate_parameters(text)
    if params:
        return f"stage_{int(stage):03d}_{slug(params[0])}"
    raise ValueError("cannot create candidate without parameter/value class")


def dry_run_fixture_group_key(frag: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Fixture-only grouping for the mandated 104/105 chi_Q dry-run episode."""
    params = set(frag.get("parameter_names") or [])
    citation = frag.get("citation") or {}
    text = str(citation.get("excerpt") or "").lower()
    has_chi_episode_param = bool(params & {"chi_Q", "Gamma_5", "sigma_Q", "matched_fingerprint_value", "outgoing_l2_DtN_fingerprint"})
    if has_chi_episode_param and ("dtn" in text or "fingerprint" in text or "chi" in text or "sigma" in text):
        return "chi_q_outgoing_dtn"
    return str(frag["candidate_key"])


def make_fragment(
    env: Env,
    *,
    modality: str,
    stage: str,
    role: str,
    path: Path,
    line: int,
    text: str,
    reason: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    params = candidate_parameters(text)
    if not params:
        raise ValueError("cannot create fragment without parameter/value class")
    return {
        "candidate_key": candidate_key_for(stage, text),
        "modality": modality,
        "anchor_stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
        "parameter_names": params,
        "reason": reason,
        "citation": {
            "path": env.rel(path),
            "line": line,
            "role": role,
            "stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
            "excerpt": text.strip(),
        },
    }


def iter_file_lines(path: Path) -> list[tuple[int, str]]:
    try:
        lines = read_text(path).splitlines()
    except OSError:
        return []
    return list(enumerate(lines, 1))


def numeric_literal_scan(env: Env, stages: list[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    literal_re = re.compile(r"(\\frac\{|\d+\s*/\s*\d+|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|=)")
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for stage in stages:
        for role, path in source_files_for_stage(env, stage):
            if role not in {"notes_stage", "paper_stage_tex"}:
                continue
            for line_no, line in iter_file_lines(path):
                if is_scaffolding_line(line):
                    continue
                if not literal_re.search(line):
                    continue
                if not has_fit_signal(line):
                    continue
                if not candidate_parameters(line):
                    continue
                out.append(
                    make_fragment(
                        env,
                        modality="numeric_literal",
                        stage=stage,
                        role=role,
                        path=path,
                        line=line_no,
                        text=line,
                        reason="target-related numerical literal or closed-form coefficient",
                    )
                )
    return out


                add(str(paper.resolve().relative_to(env.repo_root)))
            except ValueError:
                pass
    return variants


def graph_flatten_text(value: Any) -> Iterable[str]:
    if value is None:
        return
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key, child in value.items():
            yield str(key)
            yield from graph_flatten_text(child)
        return
    if isinstance(value, list):
        for child in value:
            yield from graph_flatten_text(child)
        return
    yield str(value)


def graph_normalize_terms(query: str) -> list[str]:
    return [term.lower() for term in re.split(r"\s+", query.strip()) if term]


class GraphSourceIndex:
    def __init__(self, graph: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.graph = graph
        self.nodes = graph.get("nodes") or []

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path) -> "GraphSourceIndex":
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if path.suffix.lower() in {".yaml", ".yml"}:
            return cls(yaml.safe_load(text) or {})
        return cls(yaml.safe_load(text) or {})

    def source_nodes(self, query: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        terms = graph_normalize_terms(query)
        results = []
        for node in self.nodes:
            text = " ".join(
                graph_flatten_text(
                    {
                        "file": node.get("file"),
                        "files": node.get("files"),
                        "sources": node.get("sources"),
                        "legacy_sources": node.get("legacy_sources"),
                        "legacy_file": node.get("legacy_file"),
                        "source_note_files": node.get("source_note_files"),
                        "canonical_target_files": node.get("canonical_target_files"),
                    }
                )
            ).lower()
            if text and all(term in text for term in terms):
                results.append(node)
        results.sort(key=lambda node: (node.get("layer", ""), node["id"]))
        return results


GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE: dict[Path, GraphSourceIndex] = {}


def graph_source_index(env: Env) -> GraphSourceIndex:
    graph_path = env.config_path_value("atlas_graph").resolve()
    cached = GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE.get(graph_path)
    if cached is None:
        try:
            cached = GraphSourceIndex.load(graph_path)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise SystemExit(f"error: cannot read atlas graph for source lookup: {graph_path}: {exc}") from exc
        GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE[graph_path] = cached
    return cached


def query_graph_source(env: Env, source_queries: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    index = graph_source_index(env)
    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for query in source_queries:
        nodes = index.source_nodes(query)
        if nodes:
            return nodes, []
        errors.append({"source": query, "reason": "no atlas node tied to this source path"})
    return [], errors


def graph_scan(env: Env, stages: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    fragments: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    gaps: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for stage in stages:
        for role, path in source_files_for_stage(env, stage):
            if role not in {"paper_stage_tex", "notes_stage"}:
                continue
            source_queries = source_query_variants(env, path, role, stage)
            nodes, errors = query_graph_source(env, source_queries)
            query = source_queries[0]
            if not nodes:
                gaps.append(
                    {
                        "stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
                        "source": query,
                        "graph_gap": True,
                        "attempted_sources": source_queries,
                        "reason": "; ".join(sorted({e["reason"] for e in errors})) or "no atlas node tied to this source path",
                        "stderr": next((e.get("stderr") for e in errors if e.get("stderr")), ""),
                    }
                )
                continue
            for node in nodes:
                text = " ".join(str(v) for v in node.values() if not isinstance(v, (dict, list)))
                if candidate_parameters(text):
                    fragments.append(
                        {
                            "candidate_key": candidate_key_for(stage, text),
                            "modality": "graph",
                            "anchor_stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
                            "parameter_names": candidate_parameters(text),
                            "reason": "atlas graph node carries target-related text",
                            "citation": {
                                "path": query,
                                "line": None,
                                "role": role,
                                "excerpt": node.get("id", ""),
                            },

   318	    def rel(self, path: Path) -> str:
   319	        path = path.resolve()

   321	            return str(path.relative_to(self.project_root))
   322	        except ValueError:
   323	            try:
   324	                return str(path.relative_to(self.repo_root))
   325	            except ValueError:
   326	                return str(path)

   328	    def abs_from_rel(self, path_text: str) -> Path:
   329	        p = Path(path_text)
   330	        if p.is_absolute():
   331	            return p
   332	        candidate = self.project_root / p
   333	        if candidate.exists() or not str(path_text).startswith(("docs/", "graph/")):
   334	            return candidate
   335	        return self.repo_root / p

   620	def canonical_candidate_entries(manifest: dict[str, Any], *, scanned_only: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   621	    entries = []
   622	    for entry in (manifest.get("candidates") or {}).values():
   623	        if entry.get("duplicate_of"):
   624	            continue
   625	        if scanned_only and entry.get("status") != "scanned":
   626	            continue
   627	        entries.append(entry)
   628	    return sorted(entries, key=lambda item: str(item.get("id") or ""))

   631	def stable_entry_id(prefix: str, parts: Iterable[Any]) -> str:
   632	    raw = "|".join(str(part) for part in parts)
   633	    digest = hashlib.sha1(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
   634	    return f"{prefix}_{digest}"

   637	def add_param(params: list[str], value: str | None) -> None:
   638	    if value and value not in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
   639	        params.append(value)
   640	
   641	
   642	def detect_parameters(text: str) -> list[str]:
   643	    params: list[str] = []
   644	    for match in re.finditer(r"`([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:_[A-Za-z0-9]+)+)`", text):
   645	        add_param(params, normalized_param_name(match.group(1)))
   646	    for match in re.finditer(r"\b([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:_[A-Za-z0-9]+)+)\b", text):
   647	        add_param(params, normalized_param_name(match.group(1)))
   648	    for match in re.finditer(r"\\([A-Za-z]+)(?:_\{?([^}\s^]+)\}?)?", text):
   649	        command, subscript = match.groups()
   650	        if command in TEX_COMMAND_DENYLIST:
   651	            continue
   652	        if not subscript and command in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
   653	            continue
   654	        if subscript:
   655	            add_param(params, normalized_param_name(f"{command}_{subscript}"))
   656	        elif command not in PARAMETER_DENYLIST:
   657	            add_param(params, normalized_param_name(command))
   658	    return sorted(set(params))
   659	
   660	
   661	def candidate_parameters(text: str) -> list[str]:
   662	    params = detect_parameters(text)
   663	    lower = text.lower()
   664	    has_dtn = "dtn" in lower or "fingerprint" in lower
   665	    has_value = bool(re.search(r"(\\frac\{|\d+\s*/\s*\d+|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|=)", text))
   666	    is_value_claim = has_fit_signal(text)
   667	    if has_dtn and has_value and is_value_claim:
   668	        params.append("matched_fingerprint_value")
   669	    if params and is_value_claim:
   670	        return sorted(set(params))
   671	    return []
   672	
   673	
   674	def candidate_key_for(stage: str, text: str) -> str:
   675	    params = candidate_parameters(text)
   676	    if params:
   677	        return f"stage_{int(stage):03d}_{slug(params[0])}"
   678	    raise ValueError("cannot create candidate without parameter/value class")
   679	
   680	
   681	def dry_run_fixture_group_key(frag: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   682	    """Fixture-only grouping for the mandated 104/105 chi_Q dry-run episode."""
   683	    params = set(frag.get("parameter_names") or [])
   684	    citation = frag.get("citation") or {}
   685	    text = str(citation.get("excerpt") or "").lower()
   686	    has_chi_episode_param = bool(params & {"chi_Q", "Gamma_5", "sigma_Q", "matched_fingerprint_value", "outgoing_l2_DtN_fingerprint"})
   687	    if has_chi_episode_param and ("dtn" in text or "fingerprint" in text or "chi" in text or "sigma" in text):
   688	        return "chi_q_outgoing_dtn"
   689	    return str(frag["candidate_key"])
   690	
   691	
   692	def make_fragment(
   693	    env: Env,
   694	    *,
   695	    modality: str,
   696	    stage: str,
   697	    role: str,
   698	    path: Path,
   699	    line: int,
   700	    text: str,
   701	    reason: str,
   702	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   703	    params = candidate_parameters(text)
   704	    if not params:
   705	        raise ValueError("cannot create fragment without parameter/value class")
   706	    return {
   707	        "candidate_key": candidate_key_for(stage, text),
   708	        "modality": modality,
   709	        "anchor_stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
   710	        "parameter_names": params,
   711	        "reason": reason,
   712	        "citation": {
   713	            "path": env.rel(path),
   714	            "line": line,
   715	            "role": role,
   716	            "stage": f"{int(stage):03d}",
   717	            "excerpt": text.strip(),
   718	        },
   719	    }
   720	
   721	
   722	def iter_file_lines(path: Path) -> list[tuple[int, str]]:
   723	    try:
   724	        lines = read_text(path).splitlines()
   725	    except OSError:
   726	        return []
   727	    return list(enumerate(lines, 1))
   728	
   729	
   730	def numeric_literal_scan(env: Env, stages: list[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   731	    literal_re = re.compile(r"(\\frac\{|\d+\s*/\s*\d+|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|=)")
   732	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   733	    for stage in stages:
   734	        for role, path in source_files_for_stage(env, stage):
   735	            if role not in {"notes_stage", "paper_stage_tex"}:
   736	                continue
   737	            for line_no, line in iter_file_lines(path):
   738	                if is_scaffolding_line(line):
   739	                    continue
   740	                if not literal_re.search(line):
   741	                    continue
   742	                if not has_fit_signal(line):
   743	                    continue
   744	                if not candidate_parameters(line):
   745	                    continue
   746	                out.append(
   747	                    make_fragment(
   748	                        env,
   749	                        modality="numeric_literal",
   750	                        stage=stage,
   751	                        role=role,
   752	                        path=path,
   753	                        line=line_no,
   754	                        text=line,
   755	                        reason="target-related numerical literal or closed-form coefficient",
   756	                    )
   757	                )
   758	    return out
   759

   862	
   863	def graph_flatten_text(value: Any) -> Iterable[str]:
   864	    if value is None:
   865	        return
   866	    if isinstance(value, dict):
   867	        for key, child in value.items():
   868	            yield str(key)
   869	            yield from graph_flatten_text(child)
   870	        return
   871	    if isinstance(value, list):
   872	        for child in value:
   873	            yield from graph_flatten_text(child)
   874	        return
   875	    yield str(value)
   876	
   877	
   878	def graph_normalize_terms(query: str) -> list[str]:
   879	    return [term.lower() for term in re.split(r"\s+", query.strip()) if term]
   880	
   881	
   882	class GraphSourceIndex:
   883	    def __init__(self, graph: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   884	        self.graph = graph
   885	        self.nodes = graph.get("nodes") or []
   886	
   887	    @classmethod
   888	    def load(cls, path: Path) -> "GraphSourceIndex":
   889	        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   890	        if path.suffix.lower() in {".yaml", ".yml"}:
   891	            return cls(yaml.safe_load(text) or {})
   892	        return cls(yaml.safe_load(text) or {})
   893	
   894	    def source_nodes(self, query: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   895	        terms = graph_normalize_terms(query)
   896	        results = []
   897	        for node in self.nodes:
   898	            text = " ".join(
   899	                graph_flatten_text(
   900	                    {
   901	                        "file": node.get("file"),
   902	                        "files": node.get("files"),
   903	                        "sources": node.get("sources"),
   904	                        "legacy_sources": node.get("legacy_sources"),
   905	                        "legacy_file": node.get("legacy_file"),
   906	                        "source_note_files": node.get("source_note_files"),
   907	                        "canonical_target_files": node.get("canonical_target_files"),
   908	                    }
   909	                )
   910	            ).lower()
   911	            if text and all(term in text for term in terms):
   912	                results.append(node)
   913	        results.sort(key=lambda node: (node.get("layer", ""), node["id"]))
   914	        return results
   915	
   916	
   917	GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE: dict[Path, GraphSourceIndex] = {}
   918	
   919	
   920	def graph_source_index(env: Env) -> GraphSourceIndex:
   921	    graph_path = env.config_path_value("atlas_graph").resolve()
   922	    cached = GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE.get(graph_path)
   923	    if cached is None:
   924	        try:
   925	            cached = GraphSourceIndex.load(graph_path)
   926	        except OSError as exc:
   927	            raise SystemExit(f"error: cannot read atlas graph for source lookup: {graph_path}: {exc}") from exc
   928	        GRAPH_SOURCE_INDEX_CACHE[graph_path] = cached
   929	    return cached
   930	
   931	
   932	def query_graph_source(env: Env, source_queries: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   933	    index = graph_source_index(env)
   934	    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   935	    for query in source_queries:
   936	        nodes = index.source_nodes(query)
   937	        if nodes:
   938	            return nodes, []
   939	        errors.append({"source": query, "reason": "no atlas node tied to this source path"})
   940	    return [], errors

  1844	def build_dedup_proposal(env: Env, out_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1845	    manifest = load_manifest(env)
  1846	    scanned_entries = [
  1847	        entry
  1848	        for entry in (manifest.get("candidates") or {}).values()
  1849	        if entry.get("status") == "scanned" and not entry.get("duplicate_of")
  1850	    ]
  1851	    grouped_by_stage: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
  1852	    grouped_by_stage_param: dict[tuple[str, str], list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
  1853	    for entry in scanned_entries:
  1854	        record = dedup_record(entry)
  1855	        grouped_by_stage[record["anchor_stage"]].append(record)
  1856	        for param in record["parameter_family"]:
  1857	            grouped_by_stage_param[(record["anchor_stage"], param)].append(record)
  1858	
  1859	    alias_groups: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1860	    ambiguous_by_key: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
  1861	    alias_members_by_param: set[tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
  1862	    for stage, records in sorted(grouped_by_stage.items()):
  1863	        components = connected_alias_components(records)
  1864	        for component in components:
  1865	            if len(component) < 2:
  1866	                continue
  1867	            if component_value_conflict(component):
  1868	                for param in component_parameter_family(component):
  1869	                    ambiguous_by_key[(stage, param)] = ambiguous_group_record(
  1870	                        stage,
  1871	                        param,
  1872	                        component,
  1873	                        "same stage and parameter family with adjacent citations but conflicting literal values",
  1874	                        [component],
  1875	                    )
  1876	            for alias_component in split_component_by_literal_value(component):
  1877	                if len(alias_component) < 2:
  1878	                    continue
  1879	                canonical = choose_canonical(alias_component)
  1880	                aliases = [record["id"] for record in alias_component if record["id"] != canonical["id"]]
  1881	                if not aliases:
  1882	                    continue
  1883	                all_citations = [citation for record in alias_component for citation in record.get("citations") or []]
  1884	                shared_family = component_parameter_family(alias_component)
  1885	                for record in alias_component:
  1886	                    for param in shared_family:
  1887	                        alias_members_by_param.add((stage, param, record["id"]))
  1888	                alias_groups.append(
  1889	                    {
  1890	                        "canonical_id": canonical["id"],
  1891	                        "aliases": sorted(aliases),
  1892	                        "shared": {
  1893	                            "anchor_stage": stage,
  1894	                            "parameter_family": shared_family,
  1895	                            "citation": citation_summary(all_citations),
  1896	                        },
  1897	                        "canonical_selection_rule": "most modality attributions; ties broken by candidate id sort",
  1898	                        "members": [alias_member_evidence(record["entry"]) for record in alias_component],
  1899	                    }
  1900	                )
  1901	
  1902	    for (stage, param), records in sorted(grouped_by_stage_param.items()):
  1903	        if len(records) < 2:
  1904	            continue
  1905	        grouped_ids = {record["id"] for record in records}
  1906	        aliased_ids = {cid for cid in grouped_ids if (stage, param, cid) in alias_members_by_param}
  1907	        if aliased_ids == grouped_ids:
  1908	            continue
  1909	        components = connected_alias_components(records)
  1910	        ambiguous_by_key.setdefault(
  1911	            (stage, param),
  1912	            ambiguous_group_record(
  1913	                stage,
  1914	                param,
  1915	                records,
  1916	                "same stage and parameter family but values differ or citations are outside the adjacency window; these stay separate",
  1917	                components,
  1918	            ),
  1919	        )
  1920	
  1921	    alias_groups.sort(key=lambda group: str(group["canonical_id"]))
  1922	    ambiguous = sorted(
  1923	        ambiguous_by_key.values(),
  1924	        key=lambda group: (str(group.get("anchor_stage")), "+".join(group.get("parameter_family") or [])),
  1925	    )
  1926	    total_aliases = sum(len(group.get("aliases") or []) for group in alias_groups)
  1927	    payload = {
  1928	        "schema_version": 1,
  1929	        "generated_at": now_iso(),
  1930	        "source_manifest": env.rel(env.manifest_path),
  1931	        "read_only_manifest": True,
  1932	        "rule": {
  1933	            "alias": (
  1934	                "same anchor_stage, shared normalized parameter family, and citation adjacency. Citation adjacency means "
  1935	                f"either the same normalized source path within {ALIAS_ADJACENT_LINE_WINDOW} lines (missing line numbers "
  1936	                "require identical excerpts) or stage-local citations for the same parameter whose extracted literal values do not conflict"
  1937	            ),
  1938	            "adjacent_line_window": ALIAS_ADJACENT_LINE_WINDOW,
  1939	            "canonical_selection": "highest modality-attribution count; ties by lexicographic candidate id",
  1940	            "deletion_policy": "no deletions; aliases keep manifest entries and point duplicate_of to the canonical when applied",
  1941	        },
  1942	        "summary": {
  1943	            "scanned_candidate_count": len(scanned_entries),
  1944	            "alias_group_count": len(alias_groups),
  1945	            "total_aliases": total_aliases,
  1946	            "resulting_canonical_count": len(scanned_entries) - total_aliases,
  1947	            "ambiguous_group_count": len(ambiguous),
  1948	        },
  1949	        "alias_groups": alias_groups,
  1950	        "ambiguous": ambiguous,
  1951	    }
  1952	    write_yaml(out_path, payload)
  1953	    return payload["summary"] | {"out": env.rel(out_path)}
  1954

  1956	def resolve_output_path(env: Env, path_text: str | None, default_rel: str) -> Path:
  1957	    if not path_text:
  1958	        return env.artifact_root / default_rel
  1959	    path = Path(path_text).expanduser()
  1960	    if path.is_absolute():
  1961	        return path
  1962	    return (Path.cwd() / path).resolve()

  1965	def merge_alias_into_canonical(canonical: dict[str, Any], alias: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  1966	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("anchor_stages", []), alias.get("anchor_stages") or [])
  1967	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("file_line_citations", []), alias.get("file_line_citations") or [])
  1968	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("parameter_names", []), alias.get("parameter_names") or [])
  1969	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("modality_attribution", []), alias.get("modality_attribution") or [])
  1970	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("modality_fragments", []), alias.get("modality_fragments") or [])
  1971	    append_unique_items(canonical.setdefault("alias_ids", []), [alias["id"]])
  1972	    canonical["anchor_stages"] = sorted(canonical.get("anchor_stages") or [])
  1973	    canonical["parameter_names"] = sorted(canonical.get("parameter_names") or [])
  1974	    canonical["modality_attribution"] = sorted(canonical.get("modality_attribution") or [])
  1975	    by_param = canonical.setdefault("codex_session", {}).setdefault("by_parameter", {})
  1976	    for param in canonical.get("parameter_names") or []:
  1977	        by_param.setdefault(param, new_codex_session_record())
  1978	
  1979	
  1980	def apply_alias_map(env: Env, map_path_text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1981	    source = resolve_input_path(env, map_path_text)
  1982	    data = load_yaml(source)
  1983	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
  1984	        raise SystemExit(f"error: alias map must be a YAML mapping: {env.rel(source)}")
  1985	    groups = data.get("alias_groups")
  1986	    if groups is None:
  1987	        groups = data.get("groups")
  1988	    if not isinstance(groups, list):
  1989	        raise SystemExit(f"error: alias map missing list field: alias_groups")
  1990	
  1991	    manifest = load_manifest(env)
  1992	    candidates = manifest.setdefault("candidates", {})
  1993	    errors: list[str] = []
  1994	    normalized_groups: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
  1995	    seen_aliases: dict[str, str] = {}
  1996	    for index, group in enumerate(groups, 1):
  1997	        if not isinstance(group, dict):
  1998	            errors.append(f"group[{index}] must be a mapping")

   549	def citation_line(citation: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
   550	    value = citation.get("line")
   551	    if value is None:
   552	        return None
   553	    try:
   554	        return int(value)
   555	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   556	        return None

   559	def normalize_citation_path(path_text: Any) -> str:
   560	    path = str(path_text or "").strip().replace("\\", "/")
   561	    match = re.search(r"(?:^|/)research/pde_ledger/(.+)$", path)
   562	    if match:
   563	        return match.group(1)
   564	    return path
   565	
   566	
   567	def citations_adjacent(left: dict[str, Any], right: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   568	    left_path = normalize_citation_path(left.get("path"))
   569	    right_path = normalize_citation_path(right.get("path"))
   570	    if not left_path or left_path != right_path:
   571	        return False
   572	    left_line = citation_line(left)
   573	    right_line = citation_line(right)
   574	    if left_line is not None and right_line is not None:
   575	        return abs(left_line - right_line) <= ALIAS_ADJACENT_LINE_WINDOW
   576	    if left_line is None and right_line is None:
   577	        return str(left.get("excerpt") or "") == str(right.get("excerpt") or "")
   578	    return False
   579	
   580	
   581	def entries_have_adjacent_citation(left: dict[str, Any], right: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   582	    for left_cit in left.get("file_line_citations") or []:
   583	        for right_cit in right.get("file_line_citations") or []:
   584	            if citations_adjacent(left_cit, right_cit):
   585	                return True
   586	    return False
   587	
   588	
   589	def records_value_compatible(left: dict[str, Any], right: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   590	    left_values = set(left.get("literal_values") or [])
   591	    right_values = set(right.get("literal_values") or [])
   592	    if not left_values or not right_values:
   593	        return True
   594	    return bool(left_values & right_values)
   595	
   596	
   597	def records_alias_adjacent(left: dict[str, Any], right: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   598	    if not records_share_parameter(left, right):
   599	        return False
   600	    if entries_have_adjacent_citation(left["entry"], right["entry"]):
   601	        return True
   602	    return records_value_compatible(left, right)
   603	
   604	
   605	def citation_summary(citations: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   606	    paths = sorted({normalize_citation_path(c.get("path")) for c in citations if c.get("path")})
   607	    lines = sorted({citation_line(c) for c in citations if citation_line(c) is not None})
   608	    summary: dict[str, Any] = {
   609	        "paths": paths,
   610	        "adjacency_rule": f"same path and line distance <= {ALIAS_ADJACENT_LINE_WINDOW}; missing lines require identical excerpt",
   611	    }
   612	    if len(paths) == 1:
   613	        summary["path"] = paths[0]
   614	    if lines:
   615	        summary["line_min"] = min(lines)

   506	def normalize_literal_value(text: str) -> str:
   507	    value = str(text).strip()
   508	    frac = re.fullmatch(r"\\frac\{([^{}]+)\}\{([^{}]+)\}", value)
   509	    if frac:
   510	        value = f"{frac.group(1)}/{frac.group(2)}"
   511	    value = value.replace(" ", "")
   512	    value = value.replace("\\,", "")
   513	    value = value.replace("{", "").replace("}", "")
   514	    value = value.replace("−", "-")
   515	    return value
   516	
   517	
   518	VALUE_TOKEN_RE = r"(\\frac\{[^{}]+\}\{[^{}]+\}|[-+]?\d+\s*/\s*[-+]?\d+|[-+]?\d+\.\d+(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?|[-+]?\d+(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?)"

   520	
   521	def extract_literal_values(text: Any) -> list[str]:
   522	    source = str(text or "")
   523	    values: list[str] = []
   524	    for match in re.finditer(rf"(?:=|:=|\\to|->|\\mapsto)\s*{VALUE_TOKEN_RE}", source):
   525	        value = normalize_literal_value(match.group(1))
   526	        if value not in values:
   527	            values.append(value)
   528	    for match in re.finditer(r"\\frac\{[^{}]+\}\{[^{}]+\}", source):
   529	        value = normalize_literal_value(match.group(0))
   530	        if value not in values:
   531	            values.append(value)
   532	    for match in re.finditer(r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])[-+]?\d+\s*/\s*[-+]?\d+(?![A-Za-z0-9_])", source):
   533	        value = normalize_literal_value(match.group(0))
   534	        if value not in values:
   535	            values.append(value)
   536	    return values

  1796	def component_value_conflict(component: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
  1797	    value_sets = [set(record.get("literal_values") or []) for record in component if record.get("literal_values")]
  1798	    if len(value_sets) < 2:
  1799	        return False
  1800	    return not set.intersection(*value_sets) and len(set().union(*value_sets)) > 1

  1803	def split_component_by_literal_value(component: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[list[dict[str, Any]]]:
  1804	    if not component_value_conflict(component):
  1805	        return [component]
  1806	    buckets: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
  1807	    unknown: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1808	    value_counts = Counter(value for record in component for value in (record.get("literal_values") or []))
  1809	    for record in component:
  1810	        values = record.get("literal_values") or []
  1811	        if not values:
  1812	            unknown.append(record)
  1813	            continue
  1814	        key = sorted(values, key=lambda value: (-value_counts[value], value))[0]
  1815	        buckets[key].append(record)
  1816	    if buckets and unknown:
  1817	        largest_key = sorted(buckets, key=lambda key: (-len(buckets[key]), key))[0]
  1818	        buckets[largest_key].extend(unknown)
  1819	    elif unknown:
  1820	        buckets["__no_literal_value__"].extend(unknown)
  1821	    return [sorted(records, key=lambda record: record["id"]) for _key, records in sorted(buckets.items())]
  1822	
  1823	
  1824	def choose_canonical(component: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1825	    return sorted(component, key=lambda record: (-record["modality_count"], record["id"]))[0]
  1826	
  1827	
  1828	def ambiguous_group_record(
  1829	    stage: str,
  1830	    family_key: str,
  1831	    records: list[dict[str, Any]],
  1832	    reason: str,
  1833	    components: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
  1834	) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1835	    return {
  1836	        "anchor_stage": stage,
  1837	        "parameter_family": family_key.split("+") if family_key else [],
  1838	        "reason": reason,
  1839	        "component_count": len(components or []),
  1840	        "members": [alias_member_evidence(record["entry"]) for record in sorted(records, key=lambda item: item["id"])],
  1841	    }

The implementation confirms the issue is not just bad prose in the YAML. `records_value_compatible` returns true whenever either side has no literal value, and `records_alias_adjacent` uses that as a fallback after checking shared parameter family.

from pathlib import Path
import yaml
root=Path('/var/projects/toy_physics')
dedup=yaml.safe_load((root/'research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_dedup_proposal.yaml').read_text())
ags=dedup['alias_groups']
spans=[]
for g in ags:
    c=(g.get('shared') or {}).get('citation') or {}
    if 'line_min' in c and 'line_max' in c:
        spans.append((c['line_max']-c['line_min'], g['canonical_id'], len(g.get('aliases') or [])+1, c.get('line_min'), c.get('line_max')))
print('dedup summary', dedup['summary'])
print('alias groups with line span >3', sum(1 for s in spans if s[0]>3), 'of', len(ags))
print('>=4 member groups')
for span,cid,n,lmin,lmax in sorted([s for s in spans if s[2]>=4], reverse=True)[:20]:
    print(n, cid, 'span', span, 'lines', lmin, lmax)
alias_ids=set()
for g in ags:
    alias_ids.add(g['canonical_id'])
    alias_ids.update(g.get('aliases') or [])
ambig_ids=set()
for group in dedup['ambiguous']:
    for m in group.get('members') or []:
        ambig_ids.add(m.get('id'))
overlap=sorted(alias_ids & ambig_ids)
print('alias/ambiguous overlap count', len(overlap))
print('overlap examples', overlap[:25])
fam=yaml.safe_load((root/'research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_family_map.yaml').read_text())
print('family summary', fam['summary'])
for cid in ['fit_stage_105_chi_q','fit_stage_099_k0_sym','fit_stage155_fixedpoint_expected_targets_json','fit_stage158_canonical_quartet_literals','fit_stage163_sigma0_can_digit_variant','fit_stage168_sigma0_num_digit_variant','fit_stage_168_s_can']:

# representative value 54/5 and Sigma literals

I verified the reported failures against the files. The proposals are unsafe as-is.

Key evidence:
- Dedup uses `records_value_compatible`: if either side has no extracted literals, it returns true, then `records_alias_adjacent` accepts the alias on shared parameter-family alone. See [core.py](/var/projects/toy_physics/.claude/skills/adversarial-audit/lib/core.py:589) and [core.py](/var/projects/toy_physics/.claude/skills/adversarial-audit/lib/core.py:597).
- Stage073 aliases span lines 6-94 while advertising `<= 3` line adjacency, and merge `epsilon_r`, aspect ratio, `eta`, `K_m`, `T_X`, etc. See [_dedup_proposal.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_dedup_proposal.yaml:2245).
- A second example, stage247, merges 13 members across lines 39-497: lambda_L, lambda_W, session packet values, D_UV, M_sigma, V_eff, etc. See [_dedup_proposal.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_dedup_proposal.yaml:9407).
- Parsed aggregate check: 127/140 alias groups span more than 3 lines; alias/ambiguous overlap is 153 ids.
- Family map rule is exactly param-set + each literal value, with empty-literal explicit singletons. See [_family_map.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_family_map.yaml:5) and [core.py](/var/projects/toy_physics/.claude/skills/adversarial-audit/lib/core.py:2079).
- `fit_stage_105_chi_q` maps to 18 singleton/value families, and `fit_stage_099_k0_sym` maps to 13. See [_family_map.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_family_map.yaml:1610).
- Sigma0 drift is fragmented: the 867 source exists in `fit_stage155_fixedpoint_expected_targets_json`, later 876 sources exist in stages 158/163/168, while digit-variant families are empty-value singletons. See [fit_insertion_points.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/fit_insertion_points.yaml:5880), [fit_insertion_points.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/fit_insertion_points.yaml:5995), and [_family_map.yaml](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/_family_map.yaml:19192).

## CONSULT VERDICT

Q1: AGREE. The dedup proposal is unsafe to apply. The operative failure is the value-compatible fallback plus transitive connected components, not the advertised 3-line adjacency. A false merge can absolutely hide a real fit behind another candidate’s verdict.

Q2: AGREE, with one refinement. Dedup should be strict: same stage, same single resolved target parameter, same normalized literal value, and same/nearby/prefix-equivalent citation. Drop the no-conflicting-value fallback. Also improve value extraction before relying on “empty” values, because current extraction misses forms like `sp.Float("4.651...")` and JSON colon values; if extraction still cannot confidently resolve a value, do not merge.

Q3: AGREE. Family identity should be conceptual parameter identity, with values as per-member attributes and divergences reported inside the family. Do not use the polluted `parameter_names` union as the key. Add alias normalization for concept variants such as `Sigma_0_can`, `Sigma0_can`, and `Sigma0_num`, and allow multi-family assignment only for genuinely multi-target block candidates.

Q4: AGREE with seeding named headline chains. Do not trust the re-keyed rule alone for load-bearing chains. Seed explicit named families for the D3 chains, populate mechanically via conceptual-parameter matching, and emit hard coverage warnings for expected stages/candidates missed by the rule.

Q5: DISAGREE with purely mechanical grouping-only as sufficient. Alternative: add a deterministic `primary_target_parameter` / `target_values` normalization layer, then agent-review only low-confidence cases and seeded-chain misses. The source pollution comes from Phase A scanning all parameter-like symbols in fit-signal text; see [core.py](/var/projects/toy_physics/.claude/skills/adversarial-audit/lib/core.py:642). Fix that source model or materialize the corrected target layer before rebuilding dedup/family.
